#currency demande
#Vw9mvBZGQtK3y8UeNtRD

base_demande_currency = { 
	value = scope:central_bank_scope.modifier:goods_output_liquidity_currency_add  
}
base_demande_currency_fix = { 
	value = var:base_demande_currency_fix 
}  
currency_market_goods_sell_orders_per_state = { #consomation nationale de currency dans un pays non égale à la consomation issu du marché pour les marché constitueer de pls pays
	value = 0
	every_scope_state = {
		add = sg:liquidity_currency.state_goods_consumption   
	} 
} 
currency_market_goods_sell_orders = { #réussir à ajuster la demande uniquement pour la demande de currency dans les etat que je possede
	value = currency_market_goods_sell_orders_per_state 
} 
target_demand_currency = {
    value = currency_market_goods_sell_orders
    multiply = 1 #ajuster cette vvaleur pour augmenter prix du bien au dessus de neutralité
}
target_demand_currency_ajusted = {
    value = target_demand_currency 
	# if = {
	# 	limit = {
	# 		or = {
	# 			is_subject = yes
	# 			not = {
	# 				market.owner = root
	# 			}
	# 		}
	# 	}
	# 	multiply = 0.05
	# }
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = devaluation_currency_25  } multiply = 1.25 }
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = devaluation_currency_50  } multiply = 1.50 }
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = devaluation_currency_75  } multiply = 1.75 }

	if = { limit = { has_modifier = revaluation_currency_25 } multiply = 0.75 }
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = revaluation_currency_50 } multiply = 0.50 }
	if = { limit = { has_modifier = revaluation_currency_75 } multiply = 0.25 } 

	min = 0 
}
target_demand_currency_for_modifier = {
	value = target_demand_currency_ajusted
	subtract = base_demande_currency_fix
	divide = { value = base_demande_currency_fix min = 1 }
	multiply = 100
}

	#prend en compte : GS, GES

    #dinar
	money_value_dinar_related_to_country_law = {
		value = 0

		# Si standard argent
		if = {
			limit = { has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard }
			add = money_value_dinar
			multiply = gold_to_silver_rate
		}

		# Si standard bimétallique
		if = {
			limit = { has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard }
			add = money_value_dinar
			#multiply = bimetallic_rate_gold_to_silver #retirer pour avoir la trade balance exclusivement en or 
			#la modification de cette valeur pour les pays bimetalique créer ds crach de BC par modification rapide de la balance commerciale
			#une diminution de 40% à fais fais chuter 
		}

		# Si standard or
		if = {
			limit = { 
				OR = { 
					zz_ef_exchange_std = yes
					has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
					has_law = law_type:law_no_monetary_system
					has_law = law_type:law_gold_standard 
				}
			}
			add = money_value_dinar
		}
	}

    #dollar_united_states_dollar
	money_value_dollar_united_states_dollar_related_to_country_law = {
		value = 0

		# Si standard argent
		if = {
			limit = { has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard }
			add = money_value_dollar_united_states_dollar
			multiply = gold_to_silver_rate
		}

		# Si standard bimétallique
		if = {
			limit = { has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard }
			add = money_value_dollar_united_states_dollar
			#multiply = bimetallic_rate_gold_to_silver #retirer pour avoir la trade balance exclusivement en or
		}

		# Si standard or
		if = {
			limit = { 
				OR = { 
					zz_ef_exchange_std = yes
					has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
					has_law = law_type:law_no_monetary_system
					has_law = law_type:law_gold_standard 
				}
			}
			add = money_value_dollar_united_states_dollar
		}
	}

    #krone_icelandic_krona
	money_value_krone_icelandic_krona_related_to_country_law = {
		value = 0

		# Si standard argent
		if = {
			limit = { has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard }
			add = money_value_krone_icelandic_krona
			multiply = gold_to_silver_rate
		}

		# Si standard bimétallique
		if = {
			limit = { has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard }
			add = money_value_krone_icelandic_krona
			#multiply = bimetallic_rate_gold_to_silver #retirer pour avoir la trade balance exclusivement en or
		}

		# Si standard or
		if = {
			limit = { 
				OR = { 
					zz_ef_exchange_std = yes
					has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
					has_law = law_type:law_no_monetary_system
					has_law = law_type:law_gold_standard 
				}
			}
			add = money_value_krone_icelandic_krona

		# Si standard bimétallique
		if = {
			limit = { has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard }
			add = money_value_peso_guatemalan_quetzal
			#multiply = bimetallic_rate_gold_to_silver #retirer pour avoir la trade balance exclusivement en or
		}

		# Si standard or
		if = {
			limit = { 
				OR = { 
					zz_ef_exchange_std = yes
					has_law = law_type:law_fiat_standard
					has_law = law_type:law_no_monetary_system
					has_law = law_type:law_gold_standard 
				}
			}
			add = money_value_peso_guatemalan_quetzal
		}
	}

#begin_tag_15
	############################################################################################################################################################
	#dinar
	dinar_c_market_goods_lake_ratio = {
		value = market.mg:liquidity_currency.market_goods_cheaper
		#divide = dinar_c_market_goods_buy_orders
		multiply = -1 #pour avoir une valeur positive
	}
	stockpiling_dinar_state = {
		value = 0
		add  = {
			every_scope_state = { #variable détenue dans l'etat capital
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
					}
					add = var:stockpiling_dinar_state_1
					if  = {
						limit = {
							is_set = var:stockpiling_dinar_reserve_currency_state_1
						}
						add = var:stockpiling_dinar_reserve_currency_state_1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	dinar_c_total_stokpile = {
		value = stockpiling_dinar_state
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:dinar
			}
			add = money_circulating
		} 
	}
	dinar_c_global_stokpile = {
		value = {
			add = {
				every_country = {
					if = {
						limit = {

	#lira_scudo_sardo
	lira_scudo_sardo_c_market_goods_lake_ratio = {
		value = market.mg:liquidity_currency.market_goods_cheaper
		#divide = lira_scudo_sardo_c_market_goods_buy_orders
		multiply = -1 #pour avoir une valeur positive
	}
	stockpiling_lira_scudo_sardo_state = {
		value = 0
		add  = {
			every_scope_state = { #variable détenue dans l'etat capital
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
					}
					add = var:stockpiling_lira_scudo_sardo_state_1
					if  = {
						limit = {
							is_set = var:stockpiling_lira_scudo_sardo_reserve_currency_state_1
						}
						add = var:stockpiling_lira_scudo_sardo_reserve_currency_state_1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	lira_scudo_sardo_c_total_stokpile = {
		value = stockpiling_lira_scudo_sardo_state
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:lira_scudo_sardo
			}
			add = money_circulating
		} 
	}
	lira_scudo_sardo_c_global_stokpile = {
		value = {
			add = {
				every_country = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_modifier = has_central_bank
							lira_scudo_sardo_c_total_stokpile > 0
						}
						add = lira_scudo_sardo_c_total_stokpile
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	lira_scudo_sardo_c_no_own = {
		value = lira_scudo_sardo_c_global_stokpile
		subtract = money_supply
	}

	}
	stockpiling_real_state = {
		value = 0
		add  = {
			every_scope_state = { #variable détenue dans l'etat capital
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_modifier = central_bank_historic_place
					}
					add = var:stockpiling_real_state_1
					if  = {
						limit = {
							is_set = var:stockpiling_real_reserve_currency_state_1
						}
						add = var:stockpiling_real_reserve_currency_state_1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	real_c_total_stokpile = {
		value = stockpiling_real_state
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:zz_ef_cur ?= flag:real
			}
			add = money_circulating
		} 
	}
	real_c_global_stokpile = {
		value = {
			add = {
				every_country = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_modifier = has_central_bank
							real_c_total_stokpile > 0
						}
						add = real_c_total_stokpile
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	real_c_no_own = {
		value = real_c_global_stokpile
		subtract = money_supply
	}

}

#export_in

# - j'export des goods dans un marché éttranger et je suis payé avec leur devise
# - j'accumule de la devise étrangère avec par mois export_in_dinar qui est ajouter àà la reserve de la valeur
 
export_in_gold = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit= {
			total_market_exports_quantity > 0
			export_to_gold > 0
		}
		value = export_to_gold
		multiply = export_value
		divide = total_market_exports_quantity
		multiply = money_value_target_real_in_gold
	}
}

					multiply = money_value_spe_baht_related_to_country_law 						
					if = {
						limit = { has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard }
						multiply = silver_crisis_multiplicator_factor
					} 
				}
			}
		}
   		#spe_dong

			value = {

#debt_in_national fct du standart
#brace +++++ pour l'UI
	######################################################################################################################################
	#dinar
	dinar_state_in_gold_delta = {
		value = dinar_state_in_gold
		subtract = debt_in_national_currency_dinar_in_gold
	}
	debt_in_national_currency_dinar_in_gold = { #(ce que je doit en metal )
		value = debt_in_national_currency_dinar_sc #(valeur exprimer en quoi dollar )
		multiply = money_value_target_real #_in_gold #pas in gold ducoup ????
	}
	dinar_state_in_gold = { #ce que je stock par mois
		#value = stockpiling_dinar_state
		value = var:stockpiling_dinar_c_in_reserve_currency
		multiply = money_value_dinar_related_to_country_law #money_value_dinar #(c'est ici que çà doit etre en or)
		if = {
			limit = { has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard }
			multiply = silver_crisis_multiplicator_factor
		}
	}
	######################################################################################################################################
	######################################################################################################################################

	dollar_confederate_states_dollar_state_in_gold_delta = {
		value = dollar_confederate_states_dollar_state_in_gold
		subtract = debt_in_national_currency_dollar_confederate_states_dollar_in_gold
	}
	debt_in_national_currency_dollar_confederate_states_dollar_in_gold = { #(ce que je doit en metal )
		value = debt_in_national_currency_dollar_confederate_states_dollar_sc #(valeur exprimer en quoi dollar )
		multiply = money_value_target_real #_in_gold #pas in gold ducoup ????
	}
	dollar_confederate_states_dollar_state_in_gold = { #ce que je stock par mois
		#value = stockpiling_dollar_confederate_states_dollar_state
		value = var:stockpiling_dollar_confederate_states_dollar_c_in_reserve_currency
		multiply = money_value_dollar_confederate_states_dollar_related_to_country_law #money_value_dollar_confederate_states_dollar #(c'est ici que çà doit etre en or)
		if = {
			limit = { has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard }
			multiply = silver_crisis_multiplicator_factor
		}
	}

	debt_in_national_currency_real_in_gold = { #(ce que je doit en metal )
		value = debt_in_national_currency_real_sc #(valeur exprimer en quoi dollar )
		multiply = money_value_target_real #_in_gold #pas in gold ducoup ????
	}
	real_state_in_gold = { #ce que je stock par mois
		#value = stockpiling_real_state
		value = var:stockpiling_real_c_in_reserve_currency
		multiply = money_value_real_related_to_country_law #money_value_real #(c'est ici que çà doit etre en or)
		if = {
			limit = { has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard }
			multiply = silver_crisis_multiplicator_factor
		}
	}

debt_in_national_currency_convert_gold = {  #comparaison
	value = 0
	
	#trade balance
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard
		}
		add = debt_in_national_currency_in_gold
		multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard
		}  
		add = debt_in_national_currency_in_gold 
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				has_law = law_type:law_gold_standard
				zz_ef_exchange_std = yes
			}
		}
		add = debt_in_national_currency_in_gold 
	}

}

debt_in_national_currency_convert_gold_fixe = {  #comparaison
	value = var:debt_in_national_currency_convert_gold_fix
}

	value = total_market_imports_quantity
	subtract = import_from_currency_sum

#import_in

# - j'import des goods d'un marché éttranger et je paie avec ma devise
# - j'accumule  une dette dans ma devise part type de devise étrangère donc le calcule  est libelé dans ma devise
# - cette  dette est de import_in_xx
# - cette dette se cumule dans le

#détermine la  quuantité 'u'

import_in_gold = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit= {
			total_market_imports_quantity > 0
			import_from_gold > 0
		}
		value = import_from_gold
		multiply = import_value
		divide = total_market_imports_quantity
		# multiply = money_value_dinar
		# divide = money_value_target_real_in_gold #money_value #money_value_rapported_inflation
	}
}

trade_balance_in_gold_fixe_sv = {  #stock
	value = var:trade_balance_in_gold_fixe
}
trade_balance_in_gold = {  #stock
	value = delta_in_gold
}
trade_balance_convert_gold = {  #comparaison
	value = 0
	
	#trade balance
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard
		}
		add = delta_in_gold
		multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard
		}  
		add = delta_in_gold
		#multiply = bimetallic_rate_silver_to_gold
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				has_law = law_type:law_gold_standard
				zz_ef_exchange_std = yes
			}
		}
		add = delta_in_gold 
	}

}
trade_balance_convert_gold_fixe = {  #comparaison
	value = var:trade_balance_convert_gold_fix
}

trade_balance_in_gold_delta_fix_sv = { #flux
	value = var:trade_balance_in_gold_delta_fix
}
trade_balance_in_gold_delta = { #flux
	value = export_value_in_gold_week
	subtract = import_value_in_gold_week #import_value_in_currency_week
}

excess_foreign_state_currency_convert_gold = {  #comparaison
	value = 0
	
	#trade balance
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_silver_standard
		}
		add = excess_foreign_state_currency_in_gold
		multiply = silver_to_gold_rate
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_law = law_type:law_bimetallism_standard
		}  
		add = excess_foreign_state_currency_in_gold
		#multiply = bimetallic_rate_silver_to_gold
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				has_law = law_type:law_gold_standard
				zz_ef_exchange_std = yes
			}
		}
		add = excess_foreign_state_currency_in_gold 
	} 
}
excess_foreign_state_currency_convert_gold_fixe = {  #comparaison
	value = var:excess_foreign_state_currency_convert_gold_fix
}

delta_in_gold = { #stock

#dinar
currency_identifiers_dinar = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = {
					OR = {
						#country_has_primary_culture = cu:avadhi
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:sindi
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:baluchi
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:bengali
						#country_has_primary_culture = cu:burmese
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:kachin
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:kashmiri 
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:assamese
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:oriya
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:gujarati
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:rajput
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:marathi
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:bihari
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:tamil
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:kanauji
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:sinhala
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:malayalam
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:kannada
						# country_has_primary_culture = cu:telegu
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:kanuri
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:baguirmi
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:fur
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:teda
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:bedouin
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:maghrebi
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:berber 
						country_has_primary_culture = cu:mashriqi
					} 
					NOT = {
						AND = {
							exists = c:ALD
							this = c:ALD
						} 
						AND = {
							exists = c:IRQ
							this = c:IRQ
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:EOT
							this = c:EOT
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:TRI
							this = c:TRI
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:SER
							this = c:SER
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:SER
							this = c:SER
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:TUN
							this = c:TUN
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:YUG
							this = c:YUG
						}
						AND = {
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:tuareg
						}
						AND = {
							country_has_primary_culture = cu:bidan
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:MOR
							this = c:MOR
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:OMA
							this = c:OMA
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:NEJ
							this = c:NEJ
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:HDJ
							this = c:HDJ
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:JAB
							this = c:JAB
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:ALD
							this = c:ALD
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:MAS
							this = c:MAS
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:CON
							this = c:CON
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:TUG
							this = c:TUG
						}
						AND = {
							exists = c:AIT
							this = c:AIT
						}
					}
			 
		}
		add = 1
	}
}

#dollar_caribbean_dollar
currency_identifiers_dollar_caribbean_dollar = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				AND = {
					exists = c:HAI
					this = c:HAI
				}
				AND = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:caribeno
				}
				AND = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:afro_caribeno
				}
				AND = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:afro_antillean
				}
				AND = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:afro_american
				}
				AND = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:afro_caribbean
				}
			}
			not = {
				AND = {
					exists = c:CUB
					this = c:CUB
				}
				AND = {
					exists = c:SIL
					this = c:SIL
				}
				AND = {
					exists = c:USA
					this = c:USA
				}

					country_has_primary_culture = cu:malagasy

					country_has_primary_culture = cu:yue
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:manchu
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:han
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:hakka
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:uighur
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:mongol

					country_has_primary_culture = cu:slovak
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:czech

					country_has_primary_culture = cu:maori
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:polish
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:mashriqi
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:bulgarian 
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:naga
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:tibetan
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:kho 
				}
			}
		}
		add = 1
	}
}

#thaler_prussian_thaler
currency_identifiers_thaler_prussian_thaler = {
	value = 0
	if = {
		limit = {
			or = {
				AND = {
					exists = c:PRU
					this = c:PRU
				}
				AND = {
					country_has_primary_culture = cu:north_german
				}
			}
			NOT = { 
				or = {
					AND = {
						exists = c:GER
						this = c:GER
					} 
					AND = {
						exists = c:HAN
						this = c:HAN
					} 
					AND = {
						exists = c:SAX
						this = c:SAX
					} 
					AND = {
						exists = c:LUX
						this = c:LUX
					} 
					AND = {
						exists = c:UBD
						this = c:UBD
					}  
					AND = { exists = c:HRE this = c:HRE } 
				}  
			} 
		}
		add = 1
	}
}
